According to the December 2016 data published by the SGK, the number of apprentice workers is $1m 170. This data shows that the number of children working in our country is nearing 2 million. 78% of the child workers - 558,000 of our children - are working off the books. This irregular and unsecured system, where eight of every 10 children are employed under unregistered and slave labor conditions, is causing the exploitation of children's labor to get deeper and deeper. Dear lawmakers, notice that in almost all our cities, children who are now forced to work with their tiny bodies have been added to children who have fled the war and taken refuge with their families in Turkey. Turkey has about 850,000 Syrian children in the school age. More than 490,000 Syrian children appear to be registered in schools in various parts of the country, but more than 280,000 children cannot go to school. This is also a declaration that hundreds of thousands of Syrian refugee children -- because they are missing generations -- have to live with all kinds of abuse, not protected. According to the criminal record, the number of child abuse cases has increased by 50 per cent over the past five years. The Justice Department data shows that on average 8,000 children are sexually abused a year. In January 2018 -- from the news reported to the press -- information can be found in 147 in February, 30 in February, and 269 children sexually abused in March. However, it is clearly highlighted in Article 41 of our Constitution, where each child has the right to protection and care and the state has the right to take protective measures against all abuse and violence and establish the necessary organization. Dear MPs, look, according to the Justice Ministry's August 2017 data, there are 2,800 children arrested or convicted in Turkey's penal institutions between the ages of 12 and 17 years of age. On the other hand, the number of children found in prison with their mother was 624. According to Human Rights Association data, the units related to child abuse and torture of prisoners have been registered in 2015 and 2016 as well as 10 applications in 2017. Unfortunately, despite the changing laws and practices in Turkey, there is no improvement in the situation of children within the justice system, and the problems are not reflected in the public by all its dimensions. Mr. President, the high-value lawmakers are underprivileged to protect children in Turkey. The injustices of social and economic policies also make the critical picture of children increasingly heavy. On this basis, international children's rights contracts are also being taken into consideration, and I look forward to all of our representatives, all of you, to the support of the research proposal that we've made in the context of the investigation into the problem of protecting children in our country and the possible measures to raise our children as healthy individuals.

tbmm-2018-04-24sit03spe0500par0493	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of the National Movement Party Group on the 553th Amendment proposal. On this occasion, I salute your high council. The Turkish nation saw the increasing risks and threats to our country, the games played on our backs, and the problems that could be faced in our administration system, and approved the presidency's government system on April 16th, and planned and appointed its future according to the spirit of this system. Some changes must be made in political parties and election laws in order to comply with constitutional amendments to the presidential government system. This proposal envisions some regulations in the constitutional amendment, especially in the political parties and election laws, in order to comply with changes that will take effect at the start of the calendar for the first presidential election and the start of the Turkish Grand National Assembly elections. The first article of the proposal makes arrangements to make changes in the Law on the Basic Decisions of Elections 298, so that voters registered in the foreign voter register can vote more freely. Voters registered in the foreign voter register are eligible to vote 45 days before the election day. The vote is limited to vote for the second vote of voters registered in the foreign voter register, as the second Sunday's second vote, if the first vote cannot be met with a pure majority in the presidential election. Therefore, changes are being made abroad to ensure that voting can be used until the third day before voting day, the seventh day before the voting day in Turkey. The election days set by the Supreme Election Board abroad are hereby waived the way voters are voted by appointment to allow voters to vote on any day that is appropriate for them. It is possible for political parties to report separate members of the polling board for each vote day abroad, so that the length of voting can be prevented from the challenges that have arisen in implementation. The 2nd article of the proposal is to make a change in Congressman 2839's Election Law. The new government system, which has been adopted under amendments in the Constitution, has the President's authority to run the legislation, since it will belong to the Grand National Assembly of Turkey, where no one can be a candidate for both the presidential candidate and the candidate for parliament and cannot be nominated. The amendments to the Constitution shall be made in accordance with the 3rd, 4th, 5th, and 6th amendments to the Constitution of the President's Election Law of 6271. According to the third article of the proposal, the President and Parliament vote on the same day every five years, where no one can be elected President more than twice, and if the President is re-electioned by parliament in the second term, the President can be re-electioned. The last Sunday of the vote is the day before the President and the Turkish Grand National Assembly's mandate expires, and the first day of the 60-day deadline to be determined on the basis of the previous election date held together and to be calculated backwards from the voting day is scheduled to be the start of the election. If the elections are decided to be renewed, the decision will be issued and announced in the Official Gazette in 48 hours, and the president and the Turkish Grand National Assembly's general election will be held together on Sunday, the first Sunday following the 60th day of this decision. In the event that the presidential office is ejaculated for any reason and remains in the general election for a year or less, the President's election will be held on Sunday, the first Sunday after the 60th day of the election, with the election of the Grand National Assembly of Turkey. However, in the event that the presidential office is discharged for any reason and remains in the general election for more than a year, the president's election will be held on the last Sunday in 45 days following the ejaculation. The President, elected like this, will continue his post until the election date of the Turkish Grand National Assembly, and will not be counted as a term for the President who completes the remaining deadline. According to the fourth article of the proposal, the constitution shall make the necessary provision in the second vote of the presidential election, as one of the candidates shall be granted a nomination if for any reason he does not participate in the election. In particular, it is 1700 p.m. on the day that follows the announcement of the replacement, the temporary results, in order to ensure that our citizens abroad vote. It can be done until 00. If the president is not elected in the referendum, only the election of the President will be renewed. The president's mandate will continue until the new elected President is in office, if the election is not completed, or if the election is decided to renew. If there is a president, the ruling on the dissolution of his political party is being lifted, and it is organised that if the President is elected, the parliament will end. According to Article 5 of the proposal, the Constitutional amendment would allow the presidency to nominate political parties and at least 100,000 voters, who received at least 5 per cent of the total valid vote in the last general elections held by political party groups. Under Article 6 of the proposal, political parties with a group of members in the Turkish Grand National Assembly will be able to nominate the presidency by the decision of the group, while political parties without a group will be able to run for office by the decision of the authorities in their charters. In the process of running for president by political parties, the Supreme Election Board has announced that the candidates' names and credentials and other necessary documents will be handed over to the High Election Board, and the lack of information and documents will be reported to relevant political parties, and the political parties will not be relieved of their shortcomings during the given time. In addition, each political party or party group can only apply for one candidate. The 7th article of the proposal determines the procedure and the basis for the nomination of the President to be nominated by voters for the new presidency to our legal system through a constitutional amendment. According to this, the process will begin with the application of the person wanted to be nominated by voters to the Supreme Election Board. It is decided that a receipt from the voters to the High Election Board for the ten times the monthly gross amount of payment to the highest state official as part of the financial rights and information on the application application and election competence, which contains the identity and open address information of the person who is wanted to be nominated for the President's candidate, should be submitted to the High Election Board for the interest of ten times as much as one month's gross amount. On this condition, non-serious applications are intended to promote election work. The High Election Board's refusal to submit the application if the application is determined that the application is not qualified to be elected is organised to request a re-examine within two days of this decision and to have the Supreme Election Board finalize this request within three days. If the application is lacking in information and documents, the application will be rejected if the application is not completed within two days. The Supreme Election Board is envisioned to launch the process of collecting at least 100,000 voters' signatures by announcing those whose application he has accepted. The rule is that voters can make an offer only once for one of the people mentioned in this ad. It is intended that the mayor of the district election board, which the voters have personally applied for as president of the district election board, shall have a preference among the people on the list, which has been announced to the voter after they have determined whether the person has a voter quality and whether or not he has already made a candidate offer, that the candidate offer be recorded electronically and transferred to the Higher Election Board, and that the nomination forms are physically stored in the district election boards. It is also organised that forms and information be given to the candidates. During the time set by the Supreme Election Board, it is decided that those nominated by 100,000 voters will be placed on the temporary candidate list. The application's candidacy is expected to be offered by at least 100,000 voters or dead, at the request of those involved, to return the amount invested in the Treasury register. In other cases, the amount of income will be recorded in the Treasury. In addition, the decision to appoint and take sufficient personnel on the district election boards in order to ensure that the process of running the bid quickly and effectively is determined, including the form of applications and forms and the nominations of voters registered to the foreign voter register, and the other procedures related to the application of the clause are being determined by the Supreme Election Board. Under Article 8 of the proposal, Law 298 changes, allowing voters registered in the foreign voter register to vote at any agency abroad. The amendments to the Constitutional amendments are being made to predict the election of the President and Parliament on the same day every five years, taking into account that the number of MPs is 600 and the age of parliamentary elections is 18 and there is a amendment to Congressman Election Act 2839 in order to comply with these provisions. tbmm-2018-04-24sit04spe0555par0134	Mr. President, I've been promised on behalf of the National Party of Actions to part one of Law Proposal 553. I salute you and our beloved nation. The law proposal we're meeting is only an adjustment to transition into the government system, adopted by the will of the sainted Turkish nation in the popular vote held on 16 April 2017. Dear lawmakers, the July 15th attempted treasonous coup attempt and the subsequent events have burdened the political institution with unscheduled and unscheduled burdens. In the first place, these liabilities have made the establishment of a strong administrative structure and an active Parliament structure that can be swift and swift decision-making under the state administration. The presidential government system has opened the way for Turkey, drawing a barrier with democracy's virtue and acquish to all the initiatives of its hostile evil centers. As it is known, the amendments to the Constitution's presidential and parliamentary election have been made as a result of the referendum. In this scope, it is possible that once every five years, the presidential election of the President and the MP elections could be held on the same day, if the two elections were decided to be renewed on the same day, that one person could be elected President at most twice, that political party groups could be singled out in the last parliamentary election or together to be nominated to the presidency of at least 100,000 voters. Such and similar changes also made it necessary to organise harmonisation in political parties and election laws. The proposal for the law laid out in this framework stipulates some changes in the President's Choice Act No. 6271, Congressman's Choice Act 2839, the Basic Sentences of Elections 298, and the Law on Voters Logs, and the Law on Political Parties 2820. According to this, no one will be able to run for President and not run for MP at the same time. In the five-year account of the president and members of the Turkish Grand National Assembly, the joint president and MP will be based on the election date. In the case of the presidential election's second vote, only the President's election will be renewed when the President cannot be elected, even though the vote has been reached by one candidate. If the presidential office is discharged for any reason before or after a year or so before the general election, the election of the President and the Grand National Assembly of Turkey will be held together on Sunday, the first Sunday after the 60th day. When the presidential office is free for any reason, more than a year before the general election, only the President's election will be held on the last Sunday in 45 days. If the president's mandate expires, the election can't be completed or the election is decided to be renewed, the current president's position will continue until the newly elected President is in office. If the president is elected, then the parliament will cease to exist. Each political party or party group will be searched for written representation of those who can apply for only one candidate, who will be nominated for a presidential candidate. Those who will apply to the YSK to present the presidential candidate will invest 10 times the gross amount of any payment under financial rights to the highest state official. Voters will be able to personally apply for the president of the district election board, which they are registered with. Candidates will be able to access their candidacy information in the form set by the YSK. Political parties, MPs and local governments will be able to conduct work for the election of the President as well. It also envisions arrangements for voters registered in the foreign voter register to vote more freely. In this scope, the voting period is extended until the seventh day before the election day, and the second vote in the presidential election is also taken into account until the third day before election day. Voters living abroad are allowed to vote on any day that is appropriate for them from the days of voting by appointment. Voters registered in the foreign voter register are allowed to vote at any representative abroad, allowing political parties to report separate poll board members for each vote-giving day abroad. The presidential government system, adopted by the referendum on April 16, 2017, will remove obstacles to Turkey's becoming a more democratic and stronger state within the ground of national reconciliation, making Turkey a powerful state. With the government system of the presidency, which will come to life after the elections on June 24, 2018, Turkey will spend its energy in the integration of our rich common values in Turkey's leading country and global power, instead of the dissociativeness of historical and cultural differences, where democracy has flourished and been rooted, fundamental rights and freedoms have been imposed. Therefore, the June 24, 2018 elections are a historic crossroads and turning point in the process of entering the 100th year of our republic.
